We kick off Grand National Hurdle Day with a competitive BM120 jumps event. GOLDEN CRUSADER (2) just found one too strong at Casterton last time but had been going well prior, fourth to Ongatiti here prior with that horse well fancied in the feature. This looks a nice race for him. SPLIT (1) was a smart winner at Coleraine last time in a similar sort of contest. He's coming along nicely over the obstacles and should measure up again. ZINOVATION (3) beat MORRYL MORAL (4) in a maiden hurdle at Warrnambool two back. Zinovation has since battled into second to Ferago which ties in with Golden Crusader, Morryl Moral has since run second to Flashlight who was behind Split last time. All four of those tie in quite tightly, doubt there's much between them. GRIPPING (5) is next best but doubt he can win. SUGGESTED PLAY: Golden Crusader on top.

Onto the Grand National Hurdle and ANDY WIN (1) looks clearly the one to beat. He's yet to miss the quinella in five hurdle runs, dominant at Warrnambool two back before just bumping into The Cunning Fox who is a bonafide star himself. Doubt there's anything quite of that quality here and he's not poorly in with the 68kg. ONGATITI (2) is a talented jumper. He was beaten 15 lengths by Andy Win at Warrnambool but has since won a steeplechase at Hamilton impressively. He meets Andy Win 3.5kg better off and can bridge back some of the margin. ARDAKAN (4) was dominant winning his maiden hurdle two back, sent out a $3.40 chance when third to The Cunning Fox & Andy Win last time, only a long neck behind Andy Win there. He has to be a huge chance off that. EMBEZZLER (5) was a strong Coleraine maiden hurdle winner last time and could surprise with a bit more ground. SUGGESTED PLAY: Andy Win to win.

The Grand National may be the 'feature' but I daresay most punters are looking forward to this clash in the Crisp Steeple even more. STERN IDOL (1) is seeking a record fourth win in this race having won by 25L, 10L and 15L in his three victories. Ignore the flat run last time but SING FOR PEACE (2) did clearly have Stern Idol's measure here over 3900m in the Australian Chase prior. Stern Idol had to lug a whopping 76.5kg there and now meets Sing For Peace 9.5kg better off at the weights. Sing For Peace is very good but we'll back Stern Idol to turn the tables under these conditions in what could be his farewell race. HIT THE ROAD JACK (4) is an interesting threat on resumption. He hasn't raced since finishing second in the Grand Annual over 5500m at The Bool. Stern Idol has typically had his measure over this sort of trip but he's right in the mix. MR LINCOLN (5) best of the rest along with THE MIGHTY SPAR (3). SUGGESTED PLAY: Stern Idol to bow out a winner.
